  t = (np.arange(n)- (n-1)/2) / max(1,(n-1)/2) in [-1,1]. For m in 1..d: b = t**m; b = b - b.mean(); std = np.sqrt(np.linalg.norm(b)); if std>1e-12: b/=std. Because odd/even. Good.

Smooth objective for x (array length n, mean0):
```
def _smooth_loss_and_grad_x(x, beta):
    a = np.exp(x)
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
    logc = np.log(c)
    # L1 = logsumexp(beta*logc)/beta
    m = np.max(beta*logc)
    L1 = (m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(beta*logc - m)))) / beta
    # weights
    w = np.exp(beta*logc - m) / np.sum(...)
    # grad L1 wrt x
    # compute active direct all k maybe active top? For n small, direct.
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    # We can vectorize over k: for k in range(len(c)): if w[k] > threshold: i range
    for k, wk in enumerate(w):
       if wk ==0: continue
       coeff = 2*wk / c[k]
       i0 = max(0, k-n+1); i1 = min(k, n-1)
       i = np.arange(i0, i1+1)
       j = k - i
       grad[i] += coeff * a[i] * a[j]
    # log denominator
    # Lden = logsumexp(x)
    mx = np.max(x)
    exph = np.exp(x-mx)
    S = np.sum(exph) # actual sum = exp(mx)*S
    P = exph / S
    grad -= 2.0 * P
    # since x mean0, project out constant? If x is mean0 representation, gradient from Lden and L1 may have constant component; constrained gradient = grad - mean(grad)
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    return L1 - 2*Lden, grad
```

    logc = np.log(c + tiny) + 2*xmax? For relative weights, shift cancels. Use logc_rel=np.log(c+eps)

    L1 = ... over logc_rel
    Lden = log(Ss)
    smooth_val = L1 - 2*Lden
    # weights use logc_rel

def _convolve_self(a, use_fft=True):
    n=a.size
    if use_fft and n >= 512: # maybe
       L=1
       while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
       return c[:2*n-1]
    else:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0=time.monotonic()

             update
    # But exact eval many maybe 500 * conv small, fine. Print.

`_convolve_self(a)`:
```
def _convolve_self(a):
    n=a.size
    if n < 512:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    L=1
    target=2*n-1
    while L<target: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    # maybe c[-1]=?
    return c[:target]
```
Real ifft might have tiny negative; `_score_grad` will use max for cmax and c_forlog.
For exact score, use same but `cmax = max(np.max(c),0)`.

`_fast_score_array(a)` for internal sequence a (not x):
```
n=a.size; S=sum; if S<=1e-15: inf; c=conv; return 2n*max(c,0)/S^2
```
Use for simple candidates? We can exact evaluate.

`_fast_score_x(x)`:
```
xmax=np.max(x); a=np.exp(x-xmax); S=sum; if S<=... inf; c=_convolve_self(a); cmax=max(c.max(),0); return 2n*cmax/(S*S)
```

`_score_grad_x(x,beta,active_k)` implementation details:
```
def _score_grad_x(x, beta, active_k):
    x=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: ...
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    xmax = np.max(x)
    a = np.exp(x - xmax)
    S = np.sum(a)
    if not (np.isfinite(S) and S > 1e-300): return (1e100,1e100,np.zeros(n))
    c = _convolve_self(a)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0*n*cmax/(S*S)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return ...
    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    clog = np.maximum(c, tiny)
    logc = np.log(clog)
    if active_k <= 0 or active_k >= c.size:
       idx = np.arange(c.size)
       y = logc
    else:
       k = min(active_k, c.size)
       # if all finite; use argpartition
       part = np.argpartition(-logc, k-1)[:k] if k < c.size else np.arange(c.size)
       y = logc[part]; idx=part
    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    z = beta*(y-ymax)
    # z <=0; sum exp
    w = np.exp(z)
    wsum = float(np.sum(w))
    if not finite or wsum <=0: ...
    w /= wsum
    L1 = ymax + np.log(wsum)/beta? Wait if L = LSE(beta*y) / beta: = ymax + (1/beta) log sum exp(beta*(y-ymax)). Since y max=ymax, sum exp = wsum; so L1 = ymax + log(wsum)/beta.
    Lden = np.log(S)
    val = L1 - 2*Lden
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    coeff_full = 2.0*w / clog[idx]  # length k
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        kidx = int(idx[ii]); co = float(coeff_full[ii])
        if co == 0 or not finite: continue
        i0 = kidx
        if i0 >= n? valid range: i0 = max(0, kidx - n + 1); i1 = min(kidx, n-1)
        if i0 > i1: continue
        i = np.arange(i0, i1+1)
        j = kidx - i
        grad[i] += co * a[i] * a[j]
    P = a / S
    grad -= 2.0 * P
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    if not finite grad: ...
    return val, score, grad
```
Double-check L1 formula: logsumexp(beta*y)/beta with max y=ymax => ymax + (1/beta)log∑exp(beta*(y-ymax)). We set w=exp(beta*(y-ymax))/sum, so logsum=ymax + log(sum exp)/beta = ymax + log(wsum)/beta. Good. If beta large and all equal, wsum=k, L1≈ymax+(log k)/β. Good.

Gradient coefficient: ∂val/∂x_i = ∑_k (w_k / y? derivative of L1 wrt x = ∑ w_k ∂y_k/∂x) - 2P. Since w_k = exp(beta*y_k)/Z; derivative of L1 = ∑ (w_k) ∂y_k. Actually no β because ∂ logsumexp(βy)/β = ∑ (β e^{βy}/Z)/β ∂y = ∑ w ∂y. Correct. ∂y=2a_i a_j/c. So coeff=2w/c. Good.

But if using top K and restricting y, w normalized among top; derivative ignores outside. Fine.

Now, in `_basis_objective`, if `minimize` with `jac=True` expects tuple (f, g). f can be scalar? SciPy requires f as ndarray 1D. Return `[val]`. Good.

`_basis_objective` signature includes B, beta, active_k:
```
def _basis_objective(u, B, beta, active_k):
   x = B @ u
   val, score, gx = _score_grad_x(x,beta,active_k)
   if not np.isfinite(val): return [1e100], np.zeros_like(u)
   gu = B.T @ gx
   if not finite: return [1e100], zeros
   return [val], gu
```
No score bound. `minimize` uses val only; if val large. Good.

Bases functions:
```
def _basis_polynomial(n,d):
   t = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
   cols=[]
   for m in range(1,d+1):
      b = t**m
      b = b - b.mean()
      # orthogonalize against previous? not necessary but helps condition. Use QR/covariance.
      for c in cols:
         b -= np.dot(b,c)/np.dot(c,c)*c
      norm=np.linalg.norm(b)
      if norm>1e-8: cols.append(b/norm)
   B=np.column_stack(cols)
   return B
```
This Gram-Schmidt. If not enough columns (d maybe too high for n?), pad zeros? For n=512,d=8 fine.

def _adam_refine_x(x0, deadline, beta, active_k, lr, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x-=mean
   n=x.size
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   best=x.copy(); best_score = _fast_score_x(best)
   last_best=best_score
   noimp=0
   min_lr=1e-4
   for it in range(100000):
      now=time.monotonic()
      if now > deadline: break
      val, score, g = _score_grad_x(x,beta,active_k)
      if not np.isfinite(score):
          x=best? maybe revert last; break
      if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best=x.copy(); best_score=score; last_bestency...
      if score <= last_best + 1e-12: noimp+=1
      else: last_best=score; noimp=0
      if noimp > 300:
          if lr > min_lr:
             lr *= 0.7; noimp=0
          else:
             x = x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n); x-=mean

             noimp=0
             if ... break?
      # gradient step
      t +=1
      bm1 = 1 - 0.9**t? Use constants b1=0.9,b2=0.999.

      v = 0.999*v + 0.001*g*g
      mhat = m/(1 - 0.9**t)
      vhat = v/(1 - 0.999**t)
      step = lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      x_new = x - step
      x_new -= mean

      if rng_x > 40: x_new = x_new / rng_x * 35
      if not finite: x=best? maybe break
      x=x_new
   return best, best_score

Let's code functions carefully.

`_convolve_self`:
```
def _convolve_self(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n < 512:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    target = 2*n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < target:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return c[:target]
```
For n=512 target1023, L=1024. Good.

`_fast_score_x` uses `_convolve_self`.
```
def _fast_score_x(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    xmax=np.max(x)
    a=np.exp(x-xmax)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    c=_convolve_self(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax < 0: cmax=0.0
    score=2.0*float(n)*cmax/(S*S)
    return score if np.isfinite(score) else np.inf
```

m = c.size
k = int(active_k) if active_k and active_k > 0 else 0
if k <= 0 or k >= m:
   idx=np.arange(m); y=logc
else:
   k=min(k,m)
   idx=np.argpartition(-logc, k-1)[:k]
   y=logc[idx]
```

grad[i_range] += coeff * a[i_range] * a[kidx - i_range]

`_basis_objective`: if `_minimize` returns? We'll split:

def _basis_objective(u, B, beta, active_k):
    x = np.dot(B, u)
    val, score, gx = _score_grad_x(x, beta, active_k)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return [1e100], np.zeros_like(u)
    gu = np.dot(B.T, gx)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gu)):
        return [1e100], np.zeros_like(u)
    return [val], gu
```

def _make_u_starts(d, rng, include_projection=None, project_val=None):
    starts=[]
    if project_val is not None:
       u0=np.asarray(project_val,float).copy()

       for k in range(3): starts.append(project_val + rng.normal(0,0.25,d))
    for lam in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,5.0,8.0]:
       u=np.zeros(d); u[0]=lam; starts.append(u)
       if d>1:

       u=rng.normal(0,0.6,d)

       if d>1: u[1]=rng.normal(0,0.4)

`_run_basis_search`:
```
def _run_basis_search(B, u_starts, deadline, beta, active_k, maxiter, bound):
    nvars = B.shape[1]
    best_x = None; best_s = np.inf
    bounds = [(-bound, bound)] * nvars
    for u0 in u_starts:
        u0 = np.asarray(u0, float).copy()
        if u0.size != nvars:
           tmp=np.zeros(nvars); m=min(u0.size,nvars); tmp[:m]=u0[:m]; u0=tmp
        np.clip(u0, -bound*2, bound*2, out=u0) # allow initial beyond a bit? Maybe clip to bound.
        u0 = np.clip(u0, -bound, bound)
        x0 = np.dot(B,u0)
        s0 = _fast_score_x(x0)
        if s0 < best_s: best_s=s0; best_x=x0.copy()
        if _minimize is None: continue
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        try:
            res = _minimize(_basis_objective, u0, args=(B,beta,active_k), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-8, 'gtol':1e-5})
        except Exception:
            res = None
        if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
            x1 = np.dot(B, res.x)
            x1 = x1 - np.mean(x1)
            s1 = _fast_score_x(x1)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_s:
                best_s=s1; best_x=x1.copy()
    return best_x, best_s
```
No lambdas.

Option for `minimize`: `maxls`? not necessary. Could set `maxfun` based on time? Use maxiter 60.

def _run_basis_continuation(B, x0, deadline, beta, active_k, maxiter, bound, rng):

    for k in range(3):
       starts.append(proj + rng.normal(0,0.2,nvars))
    # maybe add original starts? If x0 from lower d, maybe also default linear
    for lam in [proj[0], 0, 5]:
       u=np.zeros(nvars); u[0]=lam if nvars>0 else 0; starts.append(u)
    return _run_basis_search(B,starts,deadline,...)
```

`_adam_refine_x` if time. Need no global. Let's implement maybe used at final 4096 if no basis? We can run after 4096 basis.
```
def _adam_refine_x(x0, deadline, beta, active_k, lr, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x-=mean; n=x.size
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    best=x.copy(); best_score=np.inf
    last_best=best_score; last_best_time=time.monotonic()
    min_lr=1e-4
    for it in range(200000):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       val,score,g = _score_grad_x(x,beta,active_k)
       if not np.isfinite(score):
           x=best; break
       if score < best_score:
           best=x.copy(); best_score=score; last_best_time=time.monotonic(); last_best=score
       else:
           if score <= last_best + 1e-10 and time.monotonic()-last_best_time > 20:
               if lr > min_lr:
                  lr *= 0.7; t = max(0,t-100) # reset bias?
               else:
                  x = best.copy() + rng.normal(0,0.03,n); x-=mean
               last_best=best_score; last_best_time=time.monotonic()
       if np.all(g == 0): break
       t +=1
       b1=0.9; b2=0.999
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
       mhat = m/(1 - b1**t)
       vhat = v/(1 - b2**t)
       step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
       x_new = x - step
       x_new -= np.mean(x_new)
       rr = np.max(x_new)-np.min(x_new)
       if rr > 40:
           x_new = x_new / rr * 35.0
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
           x=best; break
       x=x_new
    return best, best_score
```
Use `best.copy() + rng.normal` safe.
If best_score inf? After first iteration set. Return.

Could `b1**t` underflow for large t; okay. t up to 10000, 0.9^10000 ~0. Use `1 - np.exp(t*np.log(b1))`? `b1**t` in Python float underflows to0, fine. For b2^t underflow.

Maybe step size lr=0.03. For basis, not need Adam.

Top-level `_prepare_x(x, max_range=35)`:
```
def _prepare_x(x, max_range=35.0):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    if x.size==0 or not finite: return None
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    rr = np.max(x)-np.min(x)
    if rr > max_range:
        x = x/rr*max_range*0.95
    return x
```

def _generate_basic_x(n, seed, max_count=60):
   rng=...
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)

   for lam in [0.5,1,1.5,2,3,5,8,12]:
      for s in [1,-1]: x=_prepare_x(s*lam*t2); if x is not None and len(out)<max_count: out.append(x)
   for p in [0.2,0.5,1,2,5,10]:
      x=_prepare_x(p*np.log(t+0.05)); ...
      x=_prepare_x(p*np.log(1-t+0.05)); ...
   for a in [1,2,4]:

      x = x - x.mean()

   return out

```
def _array_to_x(a,n):
   a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float).ravel(),0)
   S=sum; if S<=0: return None
   a=a/S
   if a.size==1: x=zeros(n)
   else:
      # use positive floor relative to max
      floor=1e-12*max(a.max(),1e-12)
      aval=np.clip(a, floor, None)
      xs=np.log(aval)
      # optional downsample by interpolation
      ts=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); tn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      xs=np.interp(tn, ts, xs)
   x=_prepare_x(xs)
   return x
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    print("starting ...", flush=True)

    # parent
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except:
       parent = np.ones(128)
    parent = np.maximum(parent,0.0)
    ps = parent.sum()
    if ps > 0:
       parent = parent / ps
       seq = parent.copy()
       if seq.size <= 6000:
          score = _evaluate_seq_array(seq)
       else:
          score = _fast_score_array(seq)
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_seq=seq
    if best_seq is None:
       seq = np.ones(128)/128; best_score=2.0; best_seq=np.ones(128)/128
    print("parent score", best_score)

    n_low=512
    d=12
    # basic
    basic = _generate_basic_x(n_low, seed)
    basic_best_x=None; basic_best_s=np.inf
    for x in basic:
       s=_fast_score_x(x)
       if s<basic_best_s: ...
    # exact top: sort? manual loop exact all? maybe 70.
    # To save, exact only those with s < best_score + 0.5? Initially best=2, s maybe<2. exact all finite? We'll exact all until time t0+30.
    exact_deadline=min(deadline,t0+60)
    for x in basic:
       if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline: break
       seq=_sequence_from_x(x); sc=_evaluate_seq_array(seq); update
    # current_x: choose best between basic_best_x and parent_x if finite
    current_x=None
    if basic_best_x is not None: current_x=basic_best_x
    # parent x

    if px is not None:
       spx=_fast_score_x(px)
       if current_x is None or spx < _fast_score_x(current_x) - 1e-12: current_x=px
    if current_x is None:
       t=np.linspace(0.2,0.8,n_low)? use x=2*(t-0.5)
       current_x=_prepare_x(2*(np.linspace(0,1,n_low)-0.5))

    # low poly
    low_deadline = min(deadline - 900.0, t0 + 600.0)
    if low_deadline < t0 + 30: low_deadline = min(deadline, t0+60)

def _sequence_from_x(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    xmax=np.max(x)
    a=np.exp(x-xmax)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if not finite or S<=1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
    return a/S
```

`_array_to_x`:
```
def _array_to_x(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<=1e-300: return None
    a=a/S
    mx=float(np.max(a))
    if mx<=0: return None
    floor=1e-12*mx
    aval=np.clip(a,floor,None)
    xs=np.log(aval)
    if a.size == 1:
       x=np.zeros(n)
    else:
       tt=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
       tn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       x=np.interp(tn,tt,xs)
    return _prepare_x(x,35.0)
```

`_generate_basic_x` as above.

Be careful with `np.linspace` for n large and n=0; n>0.

`_basis_polynomial` Gram-Schmidt:
```
def _basis_polynomial(n,d):
   t=np.linspace(-1,1,n) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
   cols=[]
   for m in range(1,d+1):
      b=t**m
      b = b - np.mean(b)
      for c in cols:
         denom=np.dot(c,c)
         if denom>1e-15:
            b = b - (np.dot(b,c)/denom)*c
      norm=np.linalg.norm(b)
      if norm > 1e-8:
         cols.append(b/norm)
      if len(cols)==d: break
   if not cols:
      return np.zeros((n,1))
   return np.column_stack(cols) if len(cols)==1 else np.column_stack(cols)
```
For d larger than cols, return with what? For n=512,d12, enough. If fewer, `_make_u_starts(d)` expects d columns; if B shape[1] < d, projection length mismatch. Ensure return exactly d by padding zeros? Better if not enough, set d_eff=len(cols) and propose d should match B.shape[1]. In propose, after building B, set `d = B.shape[1]`. Now, final code. Ensure no lambda. Use manual min/max.

Let's consider memory for B at n=4096,d=14: 57k floats, fine. For n=6000 final no B. Basic n512.

Time for `_basis_polynomial` with Gram-Schmidt loop using t**m for m up to14; for n4096, fine.

Now, one more thought: The exact evaluator computes `max_b = max(b_sequence)` where b_sequence = np.convolve(sequence, sequence). If sequence all normalized sum1, cmax ~M/n. If M continuous 0.75, cmax ~0.75/n. For n=512, score=0.75*2=1.5. Good. If continuous optimum maybe 1.4, n512 score ~1.41. Target 1.5027, so n512 enough if shape found. Good.

Now, potential that solving log-domain with x mean0 cannot represent f with total area exactly 1? Normalization handles. But basis B zero mean for log f; a constant shift of log f removed. Any positive f maps to log f - mean(log f), mean0. Good. So full representability for smooth positive.

Let's final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
except Exception:
    _minimize = None


"""
Algorithm summary:
- Represents candidate coefficients in log-domain with zero mean: a_i = exp(x_i), sum normalized later.
- Optimizes the scale-invariant objective
      2n * max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2
  using a smooth log-sum-exp approximation of the maximum convolution coefficient.
- Searches low-dimensional smooth Legendre-like bases at several resolutions,
  with L-BFGS-B when scipy is available, plus a full log-domain Adam fallback.
- Internally uses FFT convolutions; final accepted answer is validated with evaluate_sequence().
"""


def _prepare_x(x, max_range=35.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    rr = float(np.max(x) - np.min(x))
    if not np.isfinite(rr):
        return None
    if rr > max_range and rr > 0:
        x = x / rr * (0.95 * max_range)
    return x


def _sequence_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    xmax = float(np.max(x))
    a = np.exp(x - xmax)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (a / s).astype(np.float64)


def _evaluate_seq_array(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).tolist()))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _convolve_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 512:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    target = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < target:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return c[:target].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _fast_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = _convolve_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0:
        cm = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * cm / (s * s)


def _fast_score_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    xmax = float(np.max(x))
    a = np.exp(x - xmax)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = _convolve_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0:
        cm = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * cm / (s * s)


def _score_grad_x(x, beta, active_k):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    x = x - np.mean(x)
    xmax = float(np.max(x))
    a = np.exp(x - xmax)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = _convolve_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0:
        cm = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    score = 2.0 * float(n) * cm / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    clog = np.maximum(c, tiny)
    logc = np.log(clog)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logc)):
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = clog.size
    k = int(active_k) if active_k is not None else 0
    if k <= 0 or k >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        y = logc
    else:
        k = min(k, m)
        idx = np.argpartition(-logc, k - 1)[:k]
        y = logc[idx]

    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    z = beta * (y - ymax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    wz = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(wz) or wz <= 0:
        return 1e100, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = ez / wz

    l1 = ymax + float(np.log(wz)) / float(beta)
    lden = float(np.log(s))
    val = l1 - 2.0 * lden
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    coeff = (2.0 * w) / clog[idx]

    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        co = float(coeff[ii])
        if co == 0.0 or not np.isfinite(co):
            continue
        kk = int(idx[ii])
        i0 = kk
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = kk
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1
        if i0 > i1:
            continue
        i0 = max(0, kk - n + 1)
        i1 = min(kk, n - 1)
        if i0 > i1:
            continue
        ir = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        jr = kk - ir
        grad[ir] += co * a[ir] * a[jr]

    p = a / s
    grad -= 2.0 * p
    grad -= np.mean(grad)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return val, score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, score, grad


def _basis_polynomial(n, d):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros((1, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []
    for m in range(1, d + 1):
        b = t ** m
        b = b - np.mean(b)
        for c in cols:
            denom = float(np.dot(c, c))
            if denom > 1e-15:
                b = b - (float(np.dot(b, c)) / denom) * c
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(b))
        if norm > 1e-8:
            cols.append(b / norm)
        if len(cols) == d:
            break
    if not cols:
        return np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _basis_cosine(n, d):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros((1, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []
    for m in range(1, d + 1):
        b = np.cos((0.5 * (2 * m - 1)) * np.pi * t)
        b = b - np.mean(b)
        for c in cols:
            denom = float(np.dot(c, c))
            if denom > 1e-15:
                b = b - (float(np.dot(b, c)) / denom) * c
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(b))
        if norm > 1e-8:
            cols.append(b / norm)
        if len(cols) == d:
            break
    if not cols:
        return np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _interpolate_x(x_from, x_to):
    x_from = np.asarray(x_from, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_from = x_from.size
    n_to = int(x_to)
    if n_to <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n_from <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n_to, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_from == 1:
        return np.full(n_to, float(x_from[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if n_from == n_to:
        return x_from.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    tf = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_from, dtype=np.float64)
    tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_to, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.interp(tt, tf, x_from)
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    rr = float(np.max(x) - np.min(x))
    if rr > 40.0 and rr > 0:
        x = x / rr * 35.0
    return x


def _array_to_x(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a = a / s
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0:
        return None
    floor = 1e-12 * mx
    aval = np.clip(a, floor, None)
    xs = np.log(aval)
    if a.size == 1:
        x = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tf = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.interp(tt, tf, xs)
    return _prepare_x(x, 35.0)


def _generate_basic_x(n, seed, max_count=80):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = t - 0.5
    out = []

    def add(x):
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            return
        y = _prepare_x(x, 35.0)
        if y is not None:
            out.append(y)

    for lam in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 12.0]:
        for sgn in [1.0, -1.0]:
            add(sgn * lam * u)

    for p in [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0]:
        add(p * np.log(t + 0.05))
        add(p * np.log(1.0 - t + 0.05))
        add(p * np.log(t + 0.005))
        add(p * np.log(1.0 - t + 0.005))

    for a1 in [1.0, 2.0, 4.0]:
        for b1 in [-2.0, 0.0, 2.0, 4.0]:
            add(a1 * u + b1 * u * u)

    for k in range(16):
        rnd = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, n))
        rnd = rnd - np.mean(rnd)
        add(rnd + rng.normal(0.0, 2.0) * u + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * (u * u))

    return out


def _make_u_starts(d, rng, proj, bound):
    d = int(d)
    starts = []
    p = None

    if proj is not None:
        p = np.asarray(proj, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        if p.size != d:
            tmp = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(d, p.size)
            if m > 0:
                tmp[:m] = p[:m]
            p = tmp
        np.clip(p, -bound, bound, out=p)
        starts.append(p.copy())
        for _ in range(3):
            starts.append(p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, d))

    for lam in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0, -2.0, -5.0]:
        uu = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        if d > 0:
            uu[0] = lam
        starts.append(uu)

    for _ in range(10):
        rr = rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, d)
        if d > 0:
            rr[0] = abs(rr[0]) + 0.5
        if d > 1:
            rr[1] = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4)
        starts.append(rr)

    return starts


def _basis_objective(u, B, beta, active_k):
    x = np.dot(B, np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64))
    val, score, gx = _score_grad_x(x, beta, active_k)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return [1e100], np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    gu = np.dot(B.T, gx)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gu)):
        return [1e100], np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(val)], gu.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _run_basis_search(B, u_starts, deadline, beta, active_k, maxiter, bound):
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    d = int(B.shape[1]) if B.ndim == 2 else 0
    if d <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_x = None
    best_s = float("inf")
    bounds = [(-float(bound), float(bound))] * d

    for u0 in u_starts:
        u0 = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        if u0.size != d:
            tmp = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(d, u0.size)
            if m > 0:
                tmp[:m] = u0[:m]
            u0 = tmp
        np.clip(u0, -bound * 1.5, bound * 1.5, out=u0)

        x0 = np.dot(B, u0)
        x0 = x0 - np.mean(x0)
        s0 = _fast_score_x(x0)
        if np.isfinite(s0) and s0 < best_s:
            best_s = s0
            best_x = x0.copy()

        if _minimize is None:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        try:
            res = _minimize(
                _basis_objective,
                u0,
                args=(B, beta, active_k),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-9, "gtol": 1e-5, "maxls": 50},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            x1 = np.dot(B, np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            x1 = x1 - np.mean(x1)
            rr = float(np.max(x1) - np.min(x1))
            if rr > 45.0 and rr > 0:
                x1 = x1 / rr * 35.0
            s1 = _fast_score_x(x1)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_s:
                best_s = s1
                best_x = x1.copy()

    return best_x, best_s


def _run_basis_continuation(B, x0, deadline, beta, active_k, maxiter, bound, rng):
    d = int(B.shape[1]) if B.ndim == 2 else 0
    if d <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    proj = None
    if x0 is not None:
        x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x0.size == B.shape[0]:
            proj = np.dot(B.T, x0)
    starts = _make_u_starts(d, rng, proj, bound)
    return _run_basis_search(B, starts, deadline, beta, active_k, maxiter, bound)


def _adam_refine_x(x0, deadline, beta, active_k, lr, seed):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x0.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = x0.copy()
    x = x - np.mean(x)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_s = float("inf")
    last_best_time = time.monotonic()
    min_lr = 1e-4

    for _ in range(1000000):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > float(deadline):
            break

        val, score, g = _score_grad_x(x, beta, active_k)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            x = best_x.copy()
            break

        if score < best_s:
            best_s = score
            best_x = x.copy()
            last_best_time = now

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            x = best_x.copy()
            break

        if np.isfinite(best_s) and (now - last_best_time) > 35.0:
            if lr > min_lr:
                lr *= 0.65
            else:
                x = best_x.copy() + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
                x = x - np.mean(x)
            last_best_time = now

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)

        x_new = x - step
        x_new = x_new - np.mean(x_new)
        rr = float(np.max(x_new) - np.min(x_new))
        if rr > 42.0 and rr > 0:
            x_new = x_new / rr * 35.0

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
            x = best_x.copy()
            break
        x = x_new

    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        return x, float("inf")
    return best_x, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    if deadline < t0 + 1.0:
        deadline = t0 + 1.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    print("propose_candidate starting, budget_s={}".format(budget_s), flush=True)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
    ps = float(np.sum(parent))
    if ps > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
        parent = parent / ps
        if parent.size <= 6000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
            psc = _evaluate_seq_array(parent)
        else:
            psc = _fast_score_array(parent)
        if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < best_score:
            best_score = psc
            best_seq = parent.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_score = 2.0
        best_seq = np.full(128, 1.0 / 128.0, dtype=np.float64)

    print("parent score={:.6f} length={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size), flush=True)

    n_low = 512
    basic = _generate_basic_x(n_low, seed, max_count=90)
    basic_best_x = None
    basic_best_s = float("inf")

    for x in basic:
        s = _fast_score_x(x)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < basic_best_s:
            basic_best_s = s
            basic_best_x = x.copy()

    exact_basic_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 60.0)
    for x in basic:
        if time.monotonic() > exact_basic_deadline:
            break
        seq = _sequence_from_x(x)
        sc = _evaluate_seq_array(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("basic exact score={:.6f}".format(sc), flush=True)

    current_x = None
    if basic_best_x is not None:
        current_x = basic_best_x

    px = _array_to_x(best_seq, n_low)
    if px is not None:
        sp = _fast_score_x(px)
        if current_x is None:
            current_x = px
        elif np.isfinite(sp):
            scur = _fast_score_x(current_x)
            if sp < scur:
                current_x = px
                current_fast = sp
            else:
                current_fast = scur
        else:
            current_fast = _fast_score_x(current_x)
    else:
        if current_x is None:
            current_x = _prepare_x(2.0 * (np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_low) - 0.5))
        current_fast = _fast_score_x(current_x)

    if current_x is None:
        current_x = _prepare_x(2.0 * (np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_low) - 0.5))
        current_fast = _fast_score_x(current_x)

    low_deadline = min(deadline - 900.0, t0 + 600.0)
    if low_deadline < t0 + 30.0:
        low_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 60.0)

    d_low = 12
    Bpoly = _basis_polynomial(n_low, d_low)
    d_low = int(Bpoly.shape[1])
    proj = np.dot(Bpoly.T, current_x) if current_x.size == n_low else None
    starts = _make_u_starts(d_low, rng, proj, 12.0)
    poly_deadline = min(low_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.6 * max(1.0, low_deadline - time.monotonic()))
    x_res, s_res = _run_basis_search(Bpoly, starts, poly_deadline, beta=20.0, active_k=48, maxiter=80, bound=12.0)

    if x_res is not None:
        sc = _fast_score_x(x_res)
        seq = _sequence_from_x(x_res)
        esc = _evaluate_seq_array(seq)
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score:
            best_score = esc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("poly basis exact score={:.6f}".format(esc), flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_fast:
            current_x = x_res.copy()
            current_fast = sc

    if time.monotonic() < low_deadline - 15.0 and _minimize is not None:
        Bcos = _basis_cosine(n_low, d_low)
        dcos = int(Bcos.shape[1])
        projc = np.dot(Bcos.T, current_x) if current_x.size == n_low else None
        startsc = _make_u_startsc if False else _make_u_starts(dcos, rng, projc, 12.0)
        x_res2, s_res2 = _run_basis_search(Bcos, startsc, low_deadline, beta=20.0, active_k=48, maxiter=60, bound=12.0)
        if x_res2 is not None:
            seq2 = _sequence_from_x(x_res2)
            esc2 = _evaluate_seq_array(seq2)
            if np.isfinite(esc2) and esc2 < best_score:
                best_score = esc2
                best_seq = seq2.copy()
                print("cos basis exact score={:.6f}".format(esc2), flush=True)
            sc2 = _fast_score_x(x_res2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < current_fast:
                current_x = x_res2.copy()
                current_fast = sc2

    # Continue to larger resolutions in the same basis family.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    share = max(20.0, remaining / 4.0)

    stage_ns = [1024, 2048, 4096]
    for idx, n_stage in enumerate(stage_ns):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        stage_deadline = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + share)
        if stage_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 10.0:
            break

        x0 = _interpolate_x(current_x, n_stage)
        d_stage = 12 if n_stage <= 2048 else 14
        B = _basis_polynomial(n_stage, d_stage)
        if B.shape[1] <= 0:
            continue

        active_k = 48 if n_stage <= 2048 else 64
        basis_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.65 * share)
        x_stage, s_stage = _run_basis_continuation(
            B, x0, basis_deadline, beta=20.0, active_k=active_k, maxiter=70, bound=25.0, rng=rng
        )
        if x_stage is None:
            x_stage = x0
            s_stage = _fast_score_x(x_stage)

        seq_stage = _sequence_from_x(x_stage)
        sc_stage = _evaluate_seq_array(seq_stage)
        if np.isfinite(sc_stage) and sc_stage < best_score:
            best_score = sc_stage
            best_seq = seq_stage.copy()
            print("stage n={} exact score={:.6f}".format(n_stage, sc_stage), flush=True)

        # Optional fine Adam polish at this resolution.
        if time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 10.0:
            x_adam, s_adam = _adam_refine_x(
                x_stage, stage_deadline, beta=30.0, active_k=active_k,
                lr=0.02 if n_stage > 1024 else 0.03,
                seed=seed + 1000 + n_stage
            )
            if x_adam is not None:
                seq_adam = _sequence_from_x(x_adam)
                sc_adam = _evaluate_seq_array(seq_adam)
                if np.isfinite(sc_adam) and sc_adam < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_adam
                    best_seq = seq_adam.copy()
                    print("adam n={} exact score={:.6f}".format(n_stage, sc_adam), flush=True)
                sa = _fast_score_x(x_adam)
                if np.isfinite(sa) and sa < s_stage:
                    x_stage = x_adam
                    s_stage = sa

        if s_stage < current_fast:
            current_x = x_stage.copy()
            current_fast = s_stage

    # Final length sweep.
    final_tests = [1024, 2048, 4096, 6000]
    for n_test in final_tests:
        need = 20
        if n_test >= 4096:
            need = 80
        if n_test >= 6000:
            need = 140
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - float(need) + 20.0:
            continue
        x_test = _interpolate_x(current_x, n_test)
        seq_test = _sequence_from_x(x_test)
        sc_test = _evaluate_seq_array(seq_test)
        if np.isfinite(sc_test) and sc_test < best_score:
            best_score = sc_test
            best_seq = seq_test.copy()
            print("final n={} exact score={:.6f}".format(n_test, sc_test), flush=True)

    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = np.full(128, 1.0 / 128.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
    s_final = float(np.sum(best_seq))
    if not np.isfinite(s_final) or s_final <= 0:
        best_seq = np.full(128, 1.0 / 128.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / s_final
    best_seq = np.minimum(best_seq, 1000.0)

    print("returned score estimate={:.6f} length={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size), flush=True)
    return [float(v) for v in best_seq]
```
